- **brutalist-report** (styles-a) <https://brutalist.report> — work-list — Literal 1990s-web aesthetic: default blue links, black serif title, three-column plain-text headline list, dark-mode toggle as the only UI chrome — `styles-a/brutalist-report/vp-0.png`
- **cheat-sh** (styles-a) <https://cheat.sh> — — — Pure black ground, green ASCII-art logo, entire layout built from monospace box-drawing characters (+---+) instead of CSS boxes — `styles-a/cheat-sh/vp-0.png`
- **explainshell-com** (styles-a) <https://explainshell.com> — — — Minimal monospace command-explainer with a hand-drawn shell-icon logo and a clickable example list — `styles-a/explainshell-com/vp-0.png`
- **gumroad-com** (styles-a) <https://gumroad.com> — typographic-hero — Scattered pink 3D "coin" mascots tilted at random angles around a giant bold headline; thick rounded-corner black cards — `styles-a/gumroad-com/vp-0.png`
- **khanhnguyen-design** (styles-a) <https://khanhnguyen.design> — side-rail-nav, work-horizontal, typographic-hero — refs4/sections//khanhnguyen-design.png — `styles-a/khanhnguyen-design/vp-0.png`
- **once-com** (styles-a) <https://once.com> — work-grid — Flat electric-blue field, bold underlined-serif product names, pill-shaped version-number badges — `styles-a/once-com/vp-0.png`
- **storyofbrutalism-com** (styles-a) <https://storyofbrutalism.com> — typographic-hero, timeline — Giant wordmark cropped and bleeding off both left and right edges at once; GPS coordinates set in huge type as a recurring design element, not a caption — `styles-a/storyofbrutalism-com/vp-0.png`
- **takeawalkonthewildside-rietveldacademie-nl** (styles-a) <https://takeawalkonthewildside.rietveldacademie.nl> — — — The purest brutalism in the set: stretched, tiled, overlapping nature-photo collage with plain default-styled nav links floating on top in no particular alignment — `styles-a/takeawalkonthewildside-rietveldacademie-nl/vp-0.png`
- **terminal-shop** (styles-a) <https://terminal.shop> — — — The homepage is a line-numbered code editor showing the literal ssh terminal.shop command, orange blinking cursor — `styles-a/terminal-shop/vp-0.png`
- **www-craftwork-design** (styles-a) <https://craftwork.design> — typographic-hero, work-bento — Acid-yellow modal with a thick black border and hard offset shadow interrupts an otherwise plain marketplace UI — `styles-a/www-craftwork-design/vp-0.png`
- **www-hey-com** (styles-a) <https://hey.com> — typographic-hero — Chunky rounded bold type in a purple-to-pink gradient; a white rounded "card" panel floats inside a soft-gray page frame — `styles-a/www-hey-com/vp-0.png`
- **www-johnsonbanks-co-uk** (styles-a) <https://johnsonbanks.co.uk> — work-bento, logos — A single modular grid cell recurses into smaller and smaller identical grid cells (logos inside logo-tiles inside a mega-tile) — genuinely novel — `styles-a/www-johnsonbanks-co-uk/vp-0.png`

## swiss/grid

- **arturo-spatino** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://arturospatino.com> — split-hero, work-grid — Split hero (duotone portrait + serif/sans mixed headline); scroll-reveal sections currently empty until scrolled into view — good negative example of un-primed reveals — `awwwards-portfolio-a/arturo-spatino/vp-0.png`
- **gionatan-nese-26** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://gionatannese.com> — typographic-hero, process, stats, media-hero, marquee, work-bento, contact-cta — "Click to enable audio" gate; numbered nav (1 Projects / 2 About / 3 Contact) — `awwwards-portfolio-a/gionatan-nese-26/vp-0.png`
- **huy-phan-vol-2** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://huyml.co> — scroll-effect, typographic-hero — Case index with role/launch/recognition meta block per project; numbered "01/19" project counter — `awwwards-portfolio-a/huy-phan-vol-2/vp-0.png`
- **mad-monkey** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://gomadmonkey.com> — video-hero, work-list — Repetitive "SEE MORE WORK" CTA tiling a long case grid — good "avoid" example (label spam, no variation) — `awwwards-portfolio-a/mad-monkey/vp-0.png`
- **paul-kalkbrenner** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://paulkalkbrenner.net> — typographic-hero — Photo inlined inside giant wordmark type ("Paul [photo] Kalkbrenner"); persistent bottom "Now Playing" mini audio player with waveform bars and a Sound ON/OFF switch — `awwwards-portfolio-a/paul-kalkbrenner/vp-0.png`
- **signal-a-studio** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://signal-a.studio> — typographic-hero, media-hero — Vertical stacked single-letter kinetic headline (one glyph per line spelling a manifesto); dual-city live clocks (Warsaw/Berlin); each case study lists "Notable Recognitions" (Awwwards HM, CSS Winner SOTD) inline — `awwwards-portfolio-a/signal-a-studio/vp-0.png`
- **twks-1** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://twks.ch/en> — typographic-hero, about-bio, media-hero, footer-minimal, work-bento, custom-cursor, 3d-hero, services — Clean manifesto copy ("when every brand follows the same codes, they all end up looking alike"); standard but well-organized nav taxonomy (branding/campaign/digital/insights) — `awwwards-portfolio-a/twks-1/vp-0.png`
- **vilendesign** (awwwards-portfolio-a) <https://vilendesign.com> — about-bio, typographic-hero — Light/dark theme switch; case list with one-line data-viz/branding blurbs; "Archive" grid of small app-icon + thumbnail rows — `awwwards-portfolio-a/vilendesign/vp-0.png`
- **gionatannese-com** (awwwards-portfolio-b) <https://gionatannese.com> — typographic-hero — Numbered nav (1 Projects / 2 About / 3 GN.D) with an explicit "click to enable audio" gate — `awwwards-portfolio-b/gionatannese-com/vp-0.png`
- **illoca-unseen-co** (awwwards-portfolio-b) <https://illoca.unseen.co> — loader, dock-nav — Blueprint-grid background with a live cursor X/Y coordinate readout; geometric 4-icon shape preloader cycling bars/mountain/circle/triangle — `awwwards-portfolio-b/illoca-unseen-co/vp-0.png`
- **to-portfolio-com** (awwwards-portfolio-b) <https://to-portfolio.com> — process, loader — Full sci-fi terminal/boot-sequence cosplay: process IDs, timestamps, an exposed audio-routing ON/OFF toggle for ambient sound — `awwwards-portfolio-b/to-portfolio-com/vp-0.png`
- **abvtek-com** (awwwards-portfolio-c) <https://abvtek.com> — split-hero, work-grid — Conventional but clean architecture/interiors split-hero and offset gallery — `awwwards-portfolio-c/abvtek-com/vp-0.png`
- **akicreate-com** (awwwards-portfolio-c) <https://akicreate.com> — typographic-hero, contact-cta — Name hidden as the only bold letters inside a faded word-search grid of random letters — `awwwards-portfolio-c/akicreate-com/vp-0.png`
- **billchien-net-grid** (awwwards-portfolio-c) <https://billchien.net/grid> — typographic-hero, scroll-effect — One giant slab-serif letter fills the full viewport per scroll step, spelling a word letter by letter — `awwwards-portfolio-c/billchien-net-grid/vp-0.png`

- **awwwards-experience-andrey-mitko** (data-rich-portfolios) <https://awwwards.com/inspiration/experience-section-andrey-mitko-portfolio> — timeline, work-list — Clean 4-column key/value table per role (Position / Location / Industry / Website) repeated per employer — a very buildable, non-flashy resume-data layout — `data-rich-portfolios/awwwards-experience-andrey-mitko/vp-0.png`
- **brittany-chiang** (data-rich-portfolios) <https://brittanychiang.com> — split-hero, about-bio, work-list, timeline — The template thousands imitate: sticky left rail (name/nav/social) + scrolling right column with a year-range "Experience" tab (2024 — PRESENT) that reads like a resume but scrolls like a story — `data-rich-portfolios/brittany-chiang/vp-0.png`

- **lusion** (sections-and-transitions) <https://lusion.co> — marquee, fullscreen-menu, page-transition, loader — Full-screen slide-out menu with duplicated mirrored labels (marquee-style menu items) — `sections-and-transitions/lusion/vp-0.png`

- **neumorphism-clay__dribbble-com-tags-neumorphism** (styles-b-supp) <https://dribbble.com/tags/neumorphism> — work-grid — 7,212-shot gallery — mostly dashboard/app UI with soft embossed cards in both light and dark variants; use as a mood board, not a single site to clone — `styles-b-supp/neumorphism-clay__dribbble-com-tags-neumorphism/vp-0.png`
- **neumorphism-clay__duolingo-com** (styles-b-supp) <https://duolingo.com> — media-hero — Bouncy hand-illustrated 3D-clay characters (owl, chess piece, backpack) mid-tumble around a rounded phone frame; chunky pill CTAs — `styles-b-supp/neumorphism-clay__duolingo-com/vp-0.png`
- **neumorphism-clay__headspace-com** (styles-b-supp) <https://headspace.com> — media-hero — Rounded-blob orange mascot with a smile, soft cloud shapes, two large rounded-corner split panels — claymorphic brand character more than neumorphic chrome — `styles-b-supp/neumorphism-clay__headspace-com/vp-0.png`
- **neumorphism-clay__mindfulness-com** (styles-b-supp) <https://mindfulness.com> — media-hero — Borderline — floating phone mockups on a soft circular gradient backdrop; mild soft-UI feel, not distinctly neumorphic — `styles-b-supp/neumorphism-clay__mindfulness-com/vp-0.png`
- **neumorphism-clay__neumorphism-io** (styles-b-supp) <https://neumorphism.io> — — — The canonical live reference: an extruded soft-shadow card with Size/Radius/Distance/Intensity/Blur sliders and a live CSS-output panel — literally teaches the aesthetic's mechanism — `styles-b-supp/neumorphism-clay__neumorphism-io/vp-0.png`
